    WHERE u.email = @__model_Email_0
    LIMIT 1
) AS u1
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT u0.id, u0.role_id, u0.user_id, r.id AS id0, r.created_at, r.name, r.updated_at, s.id AS id1, s.permission_id, s.role_id AS role_id0, s.id0 AS id00, s.created_at AS created_at0, s.full_name, s.permission_group_id, s.short_name, s.updated_at AS updated_at0
